# Frontend contract inventory

This document also owns the P0 frontend migration acceptance ledger below. Public Seam exports and consumer targets remain canonical in `docs/frontend-seam-inventory.md`; do not maintain a second export inventory here.

This inventory defines protected frontend contracts and their current sources of truth. The completed DDD reorganization is recorded in #690 and its child issues; its migration manifest was retired in commit `7e9a3dc5` and remains available in Git history. Subsequent contract changes must be explicit and verified against the current API and owning context.

## Non-contract internals that may be renamed

TypeScript filenames, Vue component names, feature-local DTOs, adapters, composables, and internal query-key helper filenames may be renamed when all consumers are updated and the relevant tests pass. Generated API wrappers remain generated and must not be hand-edited.

## Change gate

If a change touches a protected contract, stop the directory migration and create a separate migration specification plus ADR. Do not hide semantic changes inside a move or rename.

## P0 migration acceptance freeze

Snapshot: 2026-10-10; integrator: `/root`; current checkpoint: `70e5758eddad32b588eaf641d1a470848283b0d4`. This checkpoint has no attached green CI evidence and is not an immutable migration rollback SHA. P1 execution is not authorized by this ledger.

One frozen ID identifies each TypeScript AST matcher call site in specs containing a direct `readFileSync`, `readFile(`, or `?raw` source marker. Loops/table cases are not expanded. Test titles include `it.each` and templates; repeated assertions get occurrence IDs. This scope is a syntactic candidate scan, not helper-derived dataflow analysis. Implementation layout/style constraints retire only after effective replacements. Numerical luminance/contrast and rendered behavior remain protected; tool-policy fixtures remain fixtures. All 18 undecided i18n candidates stay frozen with proposed phase and owner until tool selection, before P7. No assertion is deleted in P0.

Reproduce from `apps/web`: `npx tsx scripts/checkFrontendMigrationLedger.ts`; append `--self-test` for schema/AST fixtures. New source-read files, changed/missing matchers, changed behavior evidence and path drift produce P0 review warnings. `--strict` rejects drift for migration review; passing proves inventory consistency, not completion of pending replacements or scenarios. Each retirement needs an effective positive/negative lint fixture or observable behavior replacement before its original assertion is deleted.

| Phase | Frozen R_p | Mandatory B_p | Retirement status |
| --- | ---: | ---: | --- |
| P1a | 0 | 1 | Generated-query declaration only; explicit zero scope |
| P1b | 149 | 8 | Not started |
| P2 | 2 | 7 | Not started |
| P2.5 | 0 | 6 | Design acceptance only; explicit zero scope |
| P3 | 51 | 12 | Not started; accepted P2.5 design required |
| P4 | 4 | 5 | Not started; accepted P1 Seams required |
| P5 | 191 | 8 | Not started |
| P6 | 6 | 5 | Not started |
| P7 | 0 | 5 | Coverage/finalization only; explicit zero scope |

Measured `R=403`, plus 18 frozen i18n candidates awaiting assignment; mandatory `B=57` distinct scenarios. Twenty-five existing observable scenarios have exact mappings and local verification evidence from the full non-browser test run; 32 scenarios remain pending. Missing scenarios retain concrete expected assertions. The P3 Query trio awaits accepted P2.5 design. P7 cannot pass with pending requirements or unassigned i18n candidates.

P1b has eight planned packages totaling 56 net working hours. The working-day baseline is 2026-10-12 through 2026-10-21; these are estimates, not actual hours or completed dates. Review the schedule at explicit P1 authorization. Calendar dates do not start a lane. Actual hours, completion dates, rolling HEAD, immutable green rollback SHA and CI evidence remain distinct. Fix-forward is limited to 8 net working hours or one working day, whichever comes first; the integrator then stops assigning packages until the plan owner chooses revert or replanning.

All 680 measured `src/shared` files have destinations: 263 ordinary files are listed individually, and 417 generated files use a directory pattern, count and sorted source-path SHA-256. Generated drift verifies contents. Consumers refer to canonical Seam records when available; other files carry direct runtime/type cruiser evidence. Exact file overrides precede horizontal directory rules. Generic UI/browser/auth capabilities remain horizontal with documented reasons even when current consumers belong to one feature. All canonical CONTEXT paths are mapped; they remain English. Public export/surface/shim/fan targets belong only to `docs/frontend-seam-inventory.md`.
